{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NkYjeXJg5ga9GkEjakXLyq","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nCKA analysis for cross-lingual calibration probing (plan_011).\\\\n\\\\nComputes Linear CKA (Kornblith et al., 2019) between English and target-language\\\\nactivations, then runs four statistical analyses:\\\\n  (a) Same-layer cross-language Spearman ρ(CKA, transfer_degradation)\\\\n  (b) Partial Spearman controlling for normalized depth\\\\n  (c) Per-language Spearman across layers\\\\n  (d) Subspace angle between EN and target correctness probe directions\\\\n\\\\nInput:  Cached activations from extract_activations.py (npy format)\\\\n        activations/{model_name}/L{layer}/{lang}_{eval}.npy\\\\n        Probe metrics from train_probes.py\\\\nOutput: CKA matrix, correlation tables, bootstrap CIs, human-readable report\\\\n\\\\nDependencies: numpy, scipy, sklearn, pandas\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport pickle\\\\nimport sys\\\\nimport time\\\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport pandas as pd\\\\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr, rankdata\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Linear CKA (Kornblith et al., 2019)\\\\n# CKA(X, Y) = ||Y^T X||_F^2 / (||X^T X||_F * ||Y^T Y||_F)\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef linear_cka(X, Y):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Linear CKA between activation matrices X (n, d_x) and Y (n, d_y).\\\\n    Both must have the same n (parallel samples). Returns scalar in [0, 1].\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    X = X - X.mean(axis=0)\\\\n    Y = Y - Y.mean(axis=0)\\\\n    XTX = X.T @ X\\\\n    YTY = Y.T @ Y\\\\n    YTX = Y.T @ X\\\\n    num = np.linalg.norm(YTX, 'fro') ** 2\\\\n    den = np.linalg.norm(XTX, 'fro') * np.linalg.norm(YTY, 'fro')\\\\n    if den == 0:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n    return float(num / den)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Activation loading (npy format from extract_activations.py)\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef detect_format(act_dir):\\\\n    p = Path(act_dir)\\\\n    if list(p.glob(\\\\\\\"L*/en_eval.npy\\\\\\\")):\\\\n        return \\\\\\\"npy\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if list(p.glob(\\\\\\\"layer*_en.pt\\\\\\\")):\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": directions\\n\\nInput:  Cached activations from extract_activations.py (npy format)\\n        activations/{model_name}/L{layer}/{lang}_{eval}.npy\\n        Probe metrics from train_probes.py\\nOutput: Returns scalar in [0, 1].\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    X = X - X.mean(axis=0)\\n    Y = Y - Y.mean(axis=0)\\n    XTX = X.T @ X\\n    YTY = Y.T @ Y\\n    YTX = Y.T @ X\\n    num = np.linalg.norm(YTX, 'fro') ** 2\\n    den = np.linalg.norm(XTX, 'fro') * np.linalg.norm(YTY, 'fro')\\n    if den == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return float(num / den)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Activation loading (npy format from extract_activations.py)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef detect_format(act_dir):\\n    p = Path(act_dir)\\n    if list(p.glob(\\\"L*/en_eval.npy\\\")):\\n        return \\\"npy\\\"\\n    if list(p.glob(\\\"layer*_en.pt\\\")):\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DAsVxESCGe3XanHFd1gre8","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"def load_metrics(metrics_dir, model_name):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load EN AUROC and ZS AUROC from train_probes.py output.\\\\n    Returns: en_auroc[layer], zs_auroc[(layer, lang)]\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    en_auroc = {}\\\\n    zs_auroc = {}\\\\n\\\\n    # Try new format: results/{model_name}/metrics/transfer_matrix.json\\\\n    new_tm = Path(metrics_dir) / model_name / \\\\\\\"metrics\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\"transfer_matrix.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n    new_summary = Path(metrics_dir) / model_name / \\\\\\\"metrics\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\"summary.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if new_tm.exists():\\\\n        with open(new_tm) as f:\\\\n            tm = json.load(f)\\\\n        with open(new_summary) as f:\\\\n            summary = json.load(f)\\\\n        for key, m in summary.get(\\\\\\\"en_eval\\\\\\\", {}).items():\\\\n            # key like \\\\\\\"lr_L0\\\\\\\"\\\\n            parts = key.split(\\\\\\\"_L\\\\\\\")\\\\n            if len(parts) == 2:\\\\n                layer = int(parts[1])\\\\n                en_auroc[layer] = m[\\\\\\\"auroc\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        for pt in tm:\\\\n            for lang in tm[pt]:\\\\n                for layer_str in tm[pt][lang]:\\\\n                    layer = int(layer_str)\\\\n                    zs = tm[pt][lang][layer_str].get(\\\\\\\"zero_shot\\\\\\\")\\\\n                    if zs:\\\\n                        zs_auroc[(layer, lang)] = zs[\\\\\\\"auroc\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        return en_auroc, zs_auroc\\\\n\\\\n    # Fallback: old format CSV files\\\\n    train_csv = Path(metrics_dir) / \\\\\\\"train_metrics.csv\\\\\\\"\\\\n    transfer_csv = Path(metrics_dir) / \\\\\\\"transfer_results.csv\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if train_csv.exists() and transfer_csv.exists():\\\\n        df_train = pd.read_csv(train_csv)\\\\n        for _, row in df_train.iterrows():\\\\n            en_auroc[int(row[\\\\\\\"layer\\\\\\\"])] = row[\\\\\\\"auroc\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        df_transfer = pd.read_csv(transfer_csv)\\\\n        df_zs = df_transfer[df_transfer[\\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"zero_shot\\\\\\\"].dropna(subset=[\\\\\\\"auroc\\\\\\\"])\\\\n        for _, row in df_zs.iterrows():\\\\n            zs_auroc[(int(row[\\\\\\\"layer\\\\\\\"]), row[\\\\\\\"language\\\\\\\"])] = row[\\\\\\\"auroc\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        return en_auroc, zs_auroc\\\\n\\\\n    # Try probes_dir/train_metrics.csv\\\\n    probes_csv = Path(metrics_dir).parent / \\\\\\\"probes\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\"train_metrics.csv\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if probes_csv.exists():\\\\n        df = pd.read_csv(probes_csv)\\\\n        for _, row in df.iterrows():\\\\n            en_auroc[int(row[\\\\\\\"layer\\\\\\\"])] = row[\\\\\\\"auroc\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n    return en_auroc, zs_auroc\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_probe_weights(probes_dir, model_name, layer):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load LR probe weight vector for subspace angle analysis.\\\\n    Returns: (d,) weight vector or None.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # New format\\\\n    pkl_path = Path(probes_dir) / model_name / \\\\\\\"probes\\\\\\\" / f\\\\\\\"L{layer}\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\"en_lr_probe.pkl\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if pkl_path.exists():\\\\n        with open(pkl_path, \\\\\\\"rb\\\\\\\") as f:\\\\n            data = pickle.load(f)\\\\n        model = data[\\\\\\\"model\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        if hasattr(model, \\\\\\\"coef_\\\\\\\"):\\\\n            return model.coef_.flatten()\\\\n        return None\\\\n\\\\n    # Old format\\\\n    pkl_path = Path(probes_dir) / f\\\\\\\"probe_layer{layer}.pkl\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if pkl_path.exists():\\\\n        with open(pkl_path, \\\\\\\"rb\\\\\\\") as f:\\\\n            data = pickle.load(f)\\\\n        model = data[\\\\\\\"model\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        if hasattr(model, \\\\\\\"coef_\\\\\\\"):\\\\n            return model.coef_.flatten()\\\\n    return None\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Bootstrap CI\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef bootstrap_ci(values, stat_fn=np.mean, n_boot=1000, ci=0.95, seed=42):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Bootstrap CI for a statistic. values: array or tuple of arrays for corr.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\\\n    n = len(values) if isinstance(values, np.ndarray) else len(values[0])\\\\n    stats = []\\\\n    for _ in range(n_boot):\\\\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)\\\\n        if isinstance(values, tuple):\\\\n            s = stat_fn(values[0][idx], values[1][idx])\\\\n        else:\\\\n            s = stat_fn(values[idx])\\\\n        if s is not None and np.isfinite(s):\\\\n            stats.append(s)\\\\n    if len(stats) < 10:\\\\n        return float('nan'), float('nan')\\\\n    alpha = (1 - ci) / 2\\\\n    return float(np.percentile(stats, 100 * alpha)), float(np.percentile(stats, 100 * (1 - alpha)))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef spearman_stat(x, y):\\\\n    if len(x) < 3:\\\\n        return float('nan')\\\\n    rho, _ = spearmanr(x, y)\\\\n    return rho\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# --------------------------------\\\\n\\\\n... Matrix ---\\\")\\n    lines.append(f\\\"{'Layer':>5s}\\\" + \\\"\\\".join(f\\\" {l:>7s}\\\" for l in languages))\\n    for layer in layers:\\n        row = f\\\"{layer:>5d}\\\"\\n        for l in languages:\\n            v = cka_matrix.get((layer, l))\\n            row += f\\\" {v:>7.4f}\\\" if v is not None else f\\\" {'':>7s}\\\"\\n        lines.append(row)\\n    lines.append(\\\"\\\")\\n\\n    if same_layer_corr:\\n        lines.append(\\\"--- Same-layer cross-language Spearman ---\\\")\\n        lines.append(f\\\"{'Layer':>5s} {'n':>3s} {'ρ(CKA,deg)':>11s} {'p':>8s}\\\")\\n        for layer, v in sorted(same_layer_corr.items()):\\n            sig = \\\"*\\\" if v[\\\"p_cka_degradation\\\"] < 0.05 else \\\"\\\"\\n            lines.append(f\\\"{layer:>5d} {v['n']:>3d} {v['rho_cka_degradation']:>+10.4f}{sig}\\\"\\n                         f\\\" {v['p_cka_degradation']:>8.4f}\\\")\\n        lines.append(\\\"\\\")\\n\\n    if partial_corr_results:\\n        lines.append(\\\"--- Partial Spearman ρ(CKA, degradation | depth) ---\\\")\\n        lines.append(f\\\"  N = {partial_corr_results['n_observations']}\\\")\\n        lines.append(f\\\"  Full ρ:    {partial_corr_results['full_spearman_rho']:+.4f}\\\"\\n                     f\\\" (p={partial_corr_results['full_spearman_p']:.4f})\\\")\\n        lines.append(f\\\"  Partial ρ: {partial_corr_results['partial_spearman_rho']:+.4f}\\\"\\n                     f\\\" (p={partial_corr_results['partial_spearman_p']:.4f})\\\"\\n                     f\\\"  CI={partial_corr_results['partial_spearman_ci']}\\\")\\n        if partial_corr_results[\\\"partial_spearman_p\\\"] < 0.05:\\n            lines.append(\\\"  => CKA has independent explanatory power beyond depth\\\")\\n        else:\\n            lines.append(\\\"  => Partial ρ not significant; CKA may be a proxy for depth\\\")\\n        lines.append(\\\"\\\")\\n\\n    if per_lang_corr:\\n        lines.append(\\\"--- Per-language Spearman (CKA vs ZS AUROC) ---\\\")\\n        lines.append(f\\\"{'Lang':>6s} {'n':>3s} {'ρ':>7s} {'p':>8s}\\\")\\n        for lang, v in sorted(per_lang_corr.items()):\\n            sig = \\\"*\\\" if v[\\\"p_value\\\"] < 0.05 else \\\"\\\"\\n            lines.append(f\\\"{lang:>6s} {v['n_layers']:>3d} {v['rho']:>+7.4f} {v['p_value']:>8.4f}{sig}\\\")\\n        lines.append(\\\"\\\")\\n\\n    if subspace_angles:\\n        lines.append(\\\"--- Subspace angles (cosine similarity) ---\\\")\\n        lines.append(f\\\"{'Layer':>5s}\\\" + \\\"\\\".join(f\\\" {l:>7s}\\\" for l in languages))\\n        for layer in layers:\\n            row = f\\\"{layer:>5d}\\\"\\n            for l in languages:\\n                sa = subspace_angles.get((layer, l))\\n                row += f\\\" {sa['cosine_similarity']:>7.4f}\\\" if sa else f\\\" {'':>7s}\\\"\\n            lines.append(row)\\n        lines.append(\\\"\\\")\\n\\n    with open(out / \\\"report.txt\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        f.write(\\\"\\\\n\\\".join(lines))\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nResults saved to {args.output_dir}/\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Total elapsed: {elapsed:.1f}s\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": The `results/` dir has `transfer_results.csv` but not `train_metrics.csv`. The `probes/` dir has `train_metrics.csv` but not `transfer_results.csv`.\n\n\nThe fallback logic tries to load from the old format first, then falls back to checking the probes directory for `train_metrics.csv` and iterating through its rows to extract the AUROC values.\n\nLooking at the execution path with `--metrics_dir results`, it would skip the first two checks and successfully find the file in the probes directory, but that only populates `en_auroc` while leaving `zs_auroc` empty, which breaks the correlation analyses downstream. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01HXLrsRKED46e1ZxA24wuFH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Still running, 1:24 elapsed, 1.3GB RSS. No output yet. The high CPU usage earlier (4159%) suggests heavy numpy operations. Each CKA computation involves matrix multiplication on large matrices.\n\nLet me understand the scale:\n- 8 layers × 5 languages = 40 CKA computations\n- Each needs loading 2 .pt files (~200MB each)\n- CKA involves matrix ops on (n, d) matrices where d=3584\n\nThe pt_flat format loads with torch.load, which for 200MB files on CPU could be slow. The n is total samples; with 14042 samples in transfer results, the train split is 70% = ~9829 and eval split is ~4213. \n\nLooking at how the code loads eval activations, it reads the full tensor from disk and then slices to get the last 30%. With 200MB files per tensor, that's a lot of I/O happening repeatedly—80 times total across all language pairs, since each comparison needs both the English and target language activations.\n\nThough I realize the English activations could be reused across comparisons for the same layer, which would cut down on redundant loads. The bootstrap_ci function does:\n- 1000 resamples\n- Each resample: pick random indices, compute linear_cka on resampled data\n- linear_cka does: center, X.T @ X, Y.T @ Y, Y.T @ X, norms\n\nFor X of shape (4213, 3584):\n\n\nEach matrix multiplication in the linear_cka computation is expensive—X.T @ X alone costs around 54 billion FLOPs, and with the similar operations for Y, each bootstrap iteration runs roughly 160 billion FLOPs. Across 40 pairs with 1000 bootstraps each, that's about 6.4 × 10^15 total FLOPs. At 200 GFLOPS across all cores, this should take around 9 hours, which roughly matches what I'm seeing.\n\nBut wait—numpy's BLAS backend is much more efficient than my initial estimate. With MKL on modern hardware, each core can hit around 50 GFLOPS, so 50 cores gives 2.5 TFLOPS total. That brings the runtime down to roughly 43 minutes, which is more reasonable. The real question is whether the bootstrap is computed for each pair individually or across all pairs at once, since that changes the actual workload significantly.\n\nLooking at the output structure, it seems the bootstrap CIs are indeed computed per (layer, language) pair—so 40 separate bootstrap operations with 1000 resamples each. That's expensive, but my time estimate should still hold. The matrix multiplications for the CKA computation are the real bottleneck—each of the three products (X.T @ X, Y.T @ Y, and Y.T @ X) involves multiplying a (3584, 4213) matrix by its transpose or vice versa, each requiring around 108 billion floating-point operations. With 1000 bootstrap iterations, that's roughly 324 trillion FLOPs total, which explains why this is taking so long. Each bootstrap iteration runs through Python's for loop, where MKL handles the three matrix multiplies in parallel across all 50 cores—taking about 0.054 seconds per multiply, or 0.162 seconds total per iteration. With 1000 iterations per pair and 40 pairs, that scales to roughly 1.8 hours of compute time, though the actual runtime depends on sustained BLAS performance. The 5000% CPU usage suggests the cores are being fully utilized, so I should recalculate based on the observed wall-clock time rather than theoretical throughput. I'm working through the timing more carefully—if each CKA call takes about 0.15 seconds and I'm doing 1000 bootstrap samples per pair across 40 pairs, that's roughly 100 minutes total, which seems too long. But I'm realizing the bottleneck might not just be CKA itself; the subspace angle computation, partial correlation bootstrap, and per-language correlation bootstrap all add overhead.
